| slice_id | parent_functional_id | planned_phase | mapping_status | implementation_status | scope | implementation_files | test_modules |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| GTC/callback-only-four-owner-root-projection | GTC | Phase 1 | MATERIALIZED_CALLBACK_ONLY_OWNER_PROJECTION_API__POSITIVE_ROOT_PROJECTION_NOT_RUN | LOCALLY_VERIFIED_RECEIPT_COHERENCE_GUARDS__CALLBACK_ROOT_PROJECTION_NOT_RUN | Accepts only a typed ProgrammeMeasurementReceipt matching parent programme/attempt/input/config identity and four fresh cold F01-F04 owner roots. For g0 READY and g1 ACTIVE it is written to validate separate callback lineage and GTC transaction identities, the exact four owner receipts, before/after root hashes and generations, and callback/phase clocks, then return a noncommitting four-owner after projection. It is not the complete twelve-owner ProgrammeState and has no install or commit authority. Current focused tests cover receipt coherence and negative guards only; no positive CallbackOnlyOwnerProjection construction, Meter READY receipt, or four-owner integrated candidate set is run. | `crates/jocforge-domain/src/transaction/callback_only_root.rs`<br>`crates/jocforge-domain/src/transaction/mod.rs` | `crates/jocforge-domain/src/transaction/callback_only_root.rs` |
